import { createMcpHandler, McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/server";
import {
  parseSiteConfig,
  presets,
  SAMPLE_PHOTOS,
  SiteConfigError,
  serviceLocalGuide,
  serviceLocalSkeleton,
} from "@sites/renderer";
import { z } from "zod";
import { uploadAsset } from "./assets.js";
import type { Env } from "./env.js";
import { HttpError } from "./http.js";
import { publish, rollback } from "./publish.js";
import { addDraft, createSiteFromInput, findSite, previewUrl } from "./sites.js";
import { listVersions } from "./store.js";

type ToolResult = { content: { type: "text"; text: string }[]; structuredContent?: Record<string, unknown>; isError?: boolean };

const ok = (value: Record<string, unknown>): ToolResult => ({
  content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) }],
  structuredContent: value,
});

/** API errors become tool errors the agent can act on (validation details keep their paths). */
async function run(fn: () => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>): Promise<ToolResult> {
  try {
    return ok(await fn());
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof HttpError || error instanceof SiteConfigError) {
      const details = error instanceof HttpError ? error.details : error.errors;
      return {
        content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify({ error: error.message, details }, null, 2) }],
        isError: true,
      };
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

const configSchema = z
  .record(z.string(), z.unknown())
  .describe("사이트 설정(version 2, service-local). 형식과 작성 지침은 template_get으로 먼저 확인한다.");
const siteRef = z.string().min(1).describe("사이트 id 또는 slug");

const TEMPLATES = [
  {
    id: "service-local",
    name: "레슨·교육·출장 서비스",
    presets: Object.entries(presets).map(([id, spec]) => ({ id, label: spec.label, defaultPalette: spec.defaultPalette })),
  },
];

function createServer(env: Env) {
  const server = new McpServer({ name: "dsites", version: "1.0.0" });

  server.registerTool(
    "template_list",
    { description: "사용할 수 있는 업종 템플릿과 preset 목록." },
    async () => ok({ templates: TEMPLATES, palettes: ["pine", "ocean", "clay"] }),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "template_get",
    {
      description: "업종 템플릿의 작성 지침, 샘플 사진 키, 설정 뼈대(자리표시자). 사이트를 만들기 전에 읽는다.",
      inputSchema: z.object({ templateId: z.literal("service-local") }),
    },
    async () =>
      ok({
        templateId: "service-local",
        guide: serviceLocalGuide,
        samplePhotos: SAMPLE_PHOTOS.map((name) => `t:${name}`),
        // Placeholders only: real fixtures would leak one business's copy into another's site.
        skeleton: serviceLocalSkeleton,
      }),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "site_validate",
    {
      description: "설정을 저장하지 않고 검증한다. 오류는 경로별로 돌려준다(예: content.services.items).",
      inputSchema: z.object({ config: configSchema }),
    },
    async ({ config }) => {
      const result = parseSiteConfig(config);
      return ok(result.ok ? { valid: true, errors: [] } : { valid: false, errors: result.errors });
    },
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "site_create",
    {
      description: "새 사이트를 만든다(버전 1, 초안). 공개되지 않으며 프리뷰 URL을 돌려준다.",
      inputSchema: z.object({ config: configSchema }),
    },
    async ({ config }) =>
      run(async () => {
        const created = await createSiteFromInput(env, config, "ai");
        const versions = (await listVersions(env.DB, created.siteId)) as { preview_token: string }[];
        return { ...created, previewUrl: previewUrl(env, versions[0]!.preview_token) };
      }),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "site_get",
    {
      description: "사이트 상태, 버전 목록과 최신 버전의 설정.",
      inputSchema: z.object({ site: siteRef }),
    },
    async ({ site: ref }) =>
      run(async () => {
        const site = await findSite(env, ref);
        const versions = (await listVersions(env.DB, site.id)) as { id: string; preview_token: string }[];
        const latest = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT config FROM site_versions WHERE site_id = ? ORDER BY number DESC LIMIT 1")
          .bind(site.id)
          .first<{ config: string }>();
        return {
          site,
          versions: versions.map((v) => ({ ...v, previewUrl: previewUrl(env, v.preview_token) })),
          latestConfig: latest ? JSON.parse(latest.config) : null,
        };
      }),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "site_update_draft",
    {
      description: "수정한 설정을 새 초안 버전으로 저장한다. 공개 사이트는 바뀌지 않는다. slug는 바꿀 수 없다.",
      inputSchema: z.object({ site: siteRef, config: configSchema, note: z.string().max(200).optional() }),
    },
    async ({ site: ref, config, note }) =>
      run(async () => {
        const site = await findSite(env, ref);
        const draft = await addDraft(env, site, config, "ai", note ?? null);
        const token = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT preview_token FROM site_versions WHERE id = ?")
          .bind(draft.versionId)
          .first<{ preview_token: string }>();
        return { ...draft, previewUrl: previewUrl(env, token!.preview_token) };
      }),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "site_preview_url",
    {
      description: "버전의 프리뷰 URL(검색엔진 차단). versionId를 생략하면 최신 버전.",
      inputSchema: z.object({ site: siteRef, versionId: z.string().optional() }),
    },
    async ({ site: ref, versionId }) =>
      run(async () => {
        const site = await findSite(env, ref);
        const row = await env.DB.prepare(
          versionId
            ? "SELECT id, number, preview_token FROM site_versions WHERE site_id = ? AND id = ?"
            : "SELECT id, number, preview_token FROM site_versions WHERE site_id = ? ORDER BY number DESC LIMIT 1",
        )
          .bind(...(versionId ? [site.id, versionId] : [site.id]))
          .first<{ id: string; number: number; preview_token: string }>();
        if (!row) throw new HttpError(404, "버전이 없습니다");
        return { versionId: row.id, number: row.number, previewUrl: previewUrl(env, row.preview_token) };
      }),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "site_publish",
    {
      description: "버전을 공개한다(생략하면 최신 버전). 운영자 검수를 거친 뒤에만 호출한다. 전 세계 반영까지 약 30초.",
      inputSchema: z.object({ site: siteRef, versionId: z.string().optional() }),
    },
    async ({ site: ref, versionId }) => run(async () => ({ ...(await publish(env, await findSite(env, ref), versionId)) })),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "site_rollback",
    {
      description: "예전에 게시했던 버전으로 되돌린다. 다시 렌더하지 않는다.",
      inputSchema: z.object({ site: siteRef, versionId: z.string() }),
    },
    async ({ site: ref, versionId }) => run(async () => ({ ...(await rollback(env, await findSite(env, ref), versionId)) })),
  );

  server.registerTool(
    "asset_upload",
    {
      description:
        "사진을 올리고 설정에 넣을 key·width·height를 돌려준다. url(https, 블로그 사진 등) 또는 base64 중 하나. 3MP 이하만 받는다.",
      inputSchema: z.object({
        site: siteRef,
        url: z.string().url().optional(),
        base64: z.string().max(8_000_000).optional(),
        contentType: z.enum(["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"]).optional(),
      }),
    },
    async ({ site: ref, url, base64, contentType }) =>
      run(async () => {
        const site = await findSite(env, ref);
        if (!url === !base64) throw new HttpError(400, "url과 base64 중 하나만 주세요");
        const request = url
          ? new Request("https://internal/upload", {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({ url }),
            })
          : new Request("https://internal/upload", {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "content-type": contentType ?? "application/octet-stream" },
              body: Uint8Array.from(atob(base64!), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0)),
            });
        return { ...(await uploadAsset(env, site, request)) };
      }),
  );

  return server;
}

/** `/mcp` endpoint. The caller has passed admin auth; a fresh server serves each request. */
export function handleMcp(request: Request, env: Env) {
  return createMcpHandler(() => createServer(env)).fetch(request);
}
/**
 * Naver blog RSS gives `blogthumb.pstatic.net/...?type=s3` thumbnails (365×365). The same path
 * on `postfiles.pstatic.net` with `type=w966` serves the uploaded size (measured 900×675).
 */
export function normalizeNaverImage(url: string): string {
  const parsed = new URL(url);
  if (parsed.hostname !== "blogthumb.pstatic.net" && parsed.hostname !== "postfiles.pstatic.net") return url;
  parsed.hostname = "postfiles.pstatic.net";
  parsed.searchParams.set("type", "w966");
  return parsed.toString();
}
import { parseSiteConfig, type SiteConfig } from "@sites/renderer";
import type { Env } from "./env.js";
import { HttpError } from "./http.js";
import { syncHostRecordsNow } from "./routes.js";
import { addVersion, createSite, getSite, type CreatedBy, type SiteRow } from "./store.js";

const CREATORS: CreatedBy[] = ["ai", "operator", "customer"];

/** Parsed config with defaults filled; what gets stored. Throws 422 with every problem. */
export function validated(input: unknown): SiteConfig {
  const result = parseSiteConfig(input);
  if (!result.ok) throw new HttpError(422, "설정 검증 실패", result.errors);
  return result.config;
}

export function creator(value: unknown): CreatedBy {
  return CREATORS.includes(value as CreatedBy) ? (value as CreatedBy) : "operator";
}

/** New site with version 1 and its `<slug>.<BASE_DOMAIN>` hostname. */
export async function createSiteFromInput(env: Env, input: unknown, createdBy: CreatedBy) {
  const config = validated(input);
  const hostname = `${config.slug}.${env.BASE_DOMAIN}`;
  const { siteId, versionId } = await createSite(env.DB, config, createdBy, hostname);
  await syncHostRecordsNow(env, siteId);
  return { siteId, versionId, hostname };
}

/** New draft version; nothing goes public until it is published. */
export async function addDraft(env: Env, site: SiteRow, input: unknown, createdBy: CreatedBy, note: string | null) {
  return addVersion(env.DB, site, validated(input), createdBy, note);
}

export function previewUrl(env: Env, previewToken: string) {
  return `${env.API_ORIGIN ?? ""}/preview/${previewToken}`;
}

/** Site by id or slug (MCP callers often know only the slug). */
export async function findSite(env: Env, idOrSlug: string): Promise<SiteRow> {
  const bySlug = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM sites WHERE slug = ?").bind(idOrSlug).first<{ id: string }>();
  return getSite(env.DB, bySlug?.id ?? idOrSlug);
}

description: Cloudflare Workers best practices for production applications. Use when writing, reviewing, or configuring Workers.
---

Your knowledge of Cloudflare Workers APIs, types, and configuration may be outdated. **Prefer retrieval over pre-training** when writing or reviewing Workers code.

Use the project's installed versions, generated types, and Wrangler compatibility settings as the baseline for existing code. Retrieve relevant Cloudflare documentation to verify API, configuration, runtime behavior, and limit claims.

## References

Read the sections relevant to the task:

| Reference | When to use it |
|-----------|----------------|
| [Configuration and observability](references/configuration.md) | Compatibility dates, bindings, generated types, secrets, logs, and traces |
| [Runtime patterns](references/runtime-patterns.md) | Streaming, promise lifetime, request state, service calls, security, and runtime tests |
| [Platform API checks](references/platform-apis.md) | Handler signatures, platform classes, binding access, and serialization |

For missing evidence, consult [Workers best practices](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/best-practices/workers-best-practices/index.md) or find the affected product in the [Cloudflare docs directory](https://developers.cloudflare.com/directory/index.md). Use the installed Wrangler schema for config fields. A newer type package does not supersede the project's configured target.

## Keep Compatibility Dates Current

Use today's date for new Workers. Encourage periodic updates for existing Workers, reviewing compatibility changes and running relevant tests. Assess existing behavior against its configured date and flags; see [compatibility guidance](references/configuration.md#keep-compatibility_date-current).

## Enable Observability

Enable [Workers Logs](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/observability/logs/workers-logs/index.md) and [Traces](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/observability/traces/index.md) when creating or preparing a Worker for production. Set `observability.enabled` and `observability.traces.enabled` to `true`; the top-level setting alone does not enable traces. Use structured JSON logging and configure sampling for the workload. During reviews, flag missing logs or traces. See the [configuration example](references/configuration.md#enable-workers-logs-and-traces).

## Anti-Patterns to Flag

| Anti-pattern | Consequence and preferred pattern |
|-------------|-----------------------------------|
| `await response.text()` or similar buffering on unbounded data | Can exhaust Worker memory; [stream large or unbounded bodies](references/runtime-patterns.md#stream-request-and-response-bodies). |
| Hardcoded secrets in source or config | Leaks credentials through version control; use Wrangler secrets. |
| `Math.random()` for security-sensitive tokens or IDs | Predictable values; use `crypto.randomUUID()` or `crypto.getRandomValues()`. |
| Async work started without awaiting, returning, or attaching it to `ctx.waitUntil()` | Work can be dropped and errors missed; tie it to the request or background-work lifetime. |
| Module-level mutable request state | Leaks data across requests and can cause I/O ownership errors; pass request state explicitly. |
| Cloudflare REST API calls for operations available through Worker bindings | Adds network and authentication overhead; use the available binding. |
| `ctx.passThroughOnException()` used as general error handling | Can conceal Worker failures by forwarding to the origin; use explicit error handling and structured error responses. |
| Hand-written `Env` that duplicates Wrangler bindings | Can drift from configuration; generate binding types with `wrangler types`. |
| Direct string comparison of secret values | Can expose timing differences; use the [Web Crypto comparison pattern](references/runtime-patterns.md#use-web-crypto-for-secure-token-generation). |
| Destructuring `ctx` methods, such as `const { waitUntil } = ctx` | Loses the receiver; call `ctx.waitUntil(...)`. |
| `any` on `Env` or handler parameters | Hides binding and handler contract errors; use the project's generated and platform types. |
| `as unknown as T` to force a platform type match | Hides incompatibilities; fix the underlying contract. |
| `implements` used in place of extending a platform base class | Does not inherit runtime behavior, `this.ctx`, or `this.env`; use the appropriate base class. |
| Unbound `env.X` in a platform class method | Bindings are available through `this.env.X`; see [binding access patterns](references/platform-apis.md#binding-access--the-most-common-error). |
| Applying one serialization rule across Queues, Workflow steps, storage, and WebSockets | Can reject valid payloads or accept unsupported ones; check the [specific API and encoding](references/platform-apis.md#serialization-boundaries). |

## Validation

Use the project's existing checks for affected Workers behavior: type-check binding or handler contract changes, and run relevant runtime tests for behavior changes. Preserve required repository checks; a narrow edit does not require a full Workers audit.

## Scope

This skill covers Workers-specific best practices and code review. For related topics:

- **Durable Objects**: load the `durable-objects` skill
- **Workflows**: see [Rules of Workflows](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workflows/build/rules-of-workflows/index.md)
- **Wrangler CLI commands**: load the `wrangler` skill

// M2 step 2-7: MCP tools over Streamable HTTP with a real MCP client. An agent's whole loop:
// read the template → validate → create → upload a photo → draft → preview → publish → rollback.
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { Client, StreamableHTTPClientTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/client";
import { API, TOKEN, evidence, routerRequest, uploadShared } from "./lib/verify.mjs";

const sharp = createRequire(new URL("../packages/renderer/package.json", import.meta.url))("sharp");
const ev = evidence("m2-mcp");
const { check } = ev;
const run = Date.now().toString(36);

async function connect(token = TOKEN) {
  const client = new Client({ name: "verify-m2-mcp", version: "1.0.0" });
  const transport = new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(new URL(`${API}/mcp`), {
    requestInit: { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
  });
  await client.connect(transport);
  return client;
}
const call = async (client, name, args = {}) => {
  const result = await client.callTool({ name, arguments: args });
  const text = result.content?.[0]?.text ?? "{}";
  return { isError: Boolean(result.isError), data: result.structuredContent ?? JSON.parse(text) };
};

uploadShared();

// 1. auth
const anon = await fetch(`${API}/mcp`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: "{}" });
check("토큰 없는 MCP 요청은 401", anon.status === 401);

const client = await connect();
const { tools } = await client.listTools();
const names = tools.map((t) => t.name).sort();
const expected = [
  "asset_upload", "site_create", "site_get", "site_preview_url", "site_publish",
  "site_rollback", "site_update_draft", "site_validate", "template_get", "template_list",
].sort();
check("도구 10종 노출", JSON.stringify(names) === JSON.stringify(expected), { names });

// 2. template
const list = await call(client, "template_list");
check("template_list: service-local과 preset 2종", list.data.templates?.[0]?.presets?.length === 2);
const template = await call(client, "template_get", { templateId: "service-local" });
check(
  "template_get: 지침, 샘플 사진, 자리표시자 뼈대(실제 업체 문구 없음)",
  template.data.guide?.includes("작성 지침") && template.data.samplePhotos?.length > 5 && template.data.skeleton?.version === 2 &&
    !JSON.stringify(template.data).includes("제페스") && !JSON.stringify(template.data).includes("상쾌공간"),
);

// 3. validate (an agent's mistakes come back with paths)
const { fieldFixture } = await import("../packages/renderer/src/templates/service-local/fixtures.ts");
const draft = structuredClone(fieldFixture);
draft.slug = `mcp-${run}`;
const bad = structuredClone(draft);
bad.business.phone = "02-12";
delete bad.content.hero;
const invalid = await call(client, "site_validate", { config: bad });
check("site_validate: 경로별 오류", invalid.data.valid === false && invalid.data.errors.some((e) => e.startsWith("business.phone")) && invalid.data.errors.some((e) => e.startsWith("content.hero")), invalid.data);
check("site_validate: 올바른 설정은 valid", (await call(client, "site_validate", { config: draft })).data.valid === true);
const createBad = await call(client, "site_create", { config: bad });
check("site_create: 잘못된 설정은 도구 오류(isError)와 세부 경로", createBad.isError && JSON.stringify(createBad.data.details).includes("business.phone"));

// 4. create → preview
const created = await call(client, "site_create", { config: draft });
check("site_create: 사이트와 프리뷰 URL", !created.isError && created.data.siteId && created.data.previewUrl?.includes("/preview/"), created.data);
const preview = await fetch(created.data.previewUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+/, API));
check("프리뷰 URL 열림", preview.status === 200);

// 5. photo upload (base64) and a draft that uses it
const photo = await sharp({ create: { width: 1200, height: 800, channels: 3, background: "#3a7" } }).jpeg().toBuffer();
const uploaded = await call(client, "asset_upload", { site: draft.slug, base64: photo.toString("base64"), contentType: "image/jpeg" });
check("asset_upload(base64): key·크기", !uploaded.isError && uploaded.data.key?.startsWith("m:") && uploaded.data.width === 1200, uploaded.data);
// Live: a Naver blog RSS thumbnail (365px) is fetched at its uploaded size. Needs internet.
const thumb = await call(client, "asset_upload", {
  site: draft.slug,
  url: "https://blogthumb.pstatic.net/MjAyNjExMTBfMSAg/MDAxNzk0MzE0NzUzMDUx.g2aY3dUpdEtYHp3utLItTwaGUHclau9MLW6eU3a9eSkg.zH2Rab1Buwlc6oGfxlSVn1SknNdVve8ESmA7GFeZYCsg.JPEG/900_1788649132204.jpg?type=s3",
});
check("asset_upload(url, 외부): 네이버 RSS 썸네일을 원본 크기(900px)로", !thumb.isError && thumb.data.width === 900, thumb.data);
const both = await call(client, "asset_upload", { site: draft.slug });
check("asset_upload: url·base64 둘 다 없으면 오류", both.isError);
const v2 = structuredClone(draft);
v2.content.hero.image = { key: uploaded.data.key, alt: "현장 사진", width: uploaded.data.width, height: uploaded.data.height };
v2.content.hero.heading = "MCP로 고친\n두 번째 시안";
const updated = await call(client, "site_update_draft", { site: draft.slug, config: v2, note: "히어로 사진 교체" });
check("site_update_draft: 버전 2와 프리뷰", !updated.isError && updated.data.number === 2 && updated.data.previewUrl);
const slugChange = await call(client, "site_update_draft", { site: draft.slug, config: { ...v2, slug: `${draft.slug}-x` } });
check("site_update_draft: slug 변경은 도구 오류", slugChange.isError);
const got = await call(client, "site_get", { site: draft.slug });
check("site_get(slug): 버전 2개, 최신 설정", got.data.versions?.length === 2 && got.data.latestConfig?.content?.hero?.heading === "MCP로 고친\n두 번째 시안");
const latestPreview = await call(client, "site_preview_url", { site: draft.slug });
check("site_preview_url: 최신 버전", latestPreview.data.number === 2);

// 6. publish → live → rollback
const host = `${draft.slug}.localhost`;
const pub1 = await call(client, "site_publish", { site: draft.slug, versionId: created.data.versionId });
const pub2 = await call(client, "site_publish", { site: draft.slug });
const live = await routerRequest(host, "/");
check("site_publish: 최신(v2) 공개", !pub2.isError && live.status === 200 && live.headers["x-site-version"] === "2" && live.body.includes(uploaded.data.key.slice(2)));
const back = await call(client, "site_rollback", { site: draft.slug, versionId: pub1.data.versionId });
const afterRollback = await routerRequest(host, "/");
check("site_rollback: v1로", !back.isError && afterRollback.headers["x-site-version"] === "1");
const missing = await call(client, "site_get", { site: `nobody-${run}` });
check("없는 사이트는 도구 오류", missing.isError);

await client.close();
ev.finish({ slug: draft.slug });
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { normalizeNaverImage } from "../src/remote-image.js";

describe("normalizeNaverImage", () => {
  it("swaps RSS thumbnails for the uploaded size", () => {
    expect(normalizeNaverImage("https://blogthumb.pstatic.net/MjAy/a.JPEG/900_1.jpg?type=s3")).toBe(
      "https://postfiles.pstatic.net/MjAy/a.JPEG/900_1.jpg?type=w966",
    );
  });
  it("leaves other hosts alone", () => {
    expect(normalizeNaverImage("https://example.com/a.jpg?type=s3")).toBe("https://example.com/a.jpg?type=s3");
  });
});

import { renderSite, SiteConfigError, type SiteConfig } from "@sites/renderer";
import { listAssets, uploadAsset } from "./assets.js";
import { allInquiries, inquiriesCsv, listInquiries, requireEmail } from "./inquiries.js";
import { kstDay, metricsReport } from "./metrics.js";
import { turnstileFrame } from "./turnstile.js";
import type { Env } from "./env.js";
import { HttpError, json, readJson, requireAdmin } from "./http.js";
import { MEDIA_PATH, r2MediaKey, r2SharedKey } from "./keys.js";
import { handleMcp } from "./mcp.js";
import { assetBase, publish, rollback, siteOrigin } from "./publish.js";
import { auditHostRecords, reconcileHostRecords, syncHostRecordsNow } from "./routes.js";
import { addDraft, createSiteFromInput, creator } from "./sites.js";
import { getSite, listSites, listVersions, setSiteStatus, versionByPreviewToken, type CreatedBy } from "./store.js";

/** Must match wrangler.toml [triggers]: the hourly audit; the 5-minute cron only reconciles. */
const AUDIT_CRON = "17 * * * *";

async function serveImmutable(env: Env, key: string) {
  const object = await env.SITES.get(key);
  if (!object) return new Response("not found", { status: 404 });
  const headers = new Headers();
  object.writeHttpMetadata(headers);
  headers.set("cache-control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable");
  return new Response(object.body, { headers });
}

async function handle(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const parts = url.pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean);
  const method = request.method;

  if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") return json({ ok: true });

  // Shared assets are public and immutable; previews load them from here.
  if (method === "GET" && parts[0] === "_shared") return serveImmutable(env, r2SharedKey(parts.slice(1).join("/")));
  // Uploaded photos (public, content-addressed) so previews show them before publishing.
  const media = method === "GET" ? MEDIA_PATH.exec(url.pathname) : null;
  if (media) return serveImmutable(env, r2MediaKey(media[1]!, media[2]!));

  // Turnstile widget frame embedded by public sites (see turnstile.ts).
  if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/turnstile-frame") {
    if (!env.TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY) return new Response("turnstile not configured", { status: 404 });
    const parent = url.searchParams.get("parent") ?? "";
    if (!/^https?:\/\/[a-z0-9.-]+(:\d+)?$/i.test(parent)) return new Response("bad parent", { status: 400 });
    return new Response(turnstileFrame(env.TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY, parent), {
      headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store", "x-robots-tag": "noindex" },
    });
  }

  // Preview: anyone with the token can view a version. Never indexed. /preview/<token>[/privacy]
  if (method === "GET" && parts[0] === "preview" && (parts.length === 2 || parts.length === 3)) {
    const version = await versionByPreviewToken(env.DB, parts[1]!);
    const site = await getSite(env.DB, version.site_id);
    const { pages } = await renderSite(JSON.parse(version.config) as SiteConfig, {
      assetBase: assetBase(),
      origin: await siteOrigin(env, site),
      preview: true,
      turnstileFrame: null,
      // Site links are root-relative (`/#inquiry`, `/privacy`); keep them inside this preview.
      linkPrefix: `/preview/${parts[1]}`,
    });
    const route = parts[2] ? `/${parts[2]}` : "/";
    const page = pages.find((entry) => entry.route === route);
    if (!page) throw new HttpError(404, "없는 페이지입니다");
    return new Response(page.html, {
      headers: {
        "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
        "x-robots-tag": "noindex, nofollow",
        "cache-control": "no-store",
      },
    });
  }

  requireAdmin(request, env.ADMIN_TOKEN);

  // MCP tools for AI agents (Streamable HTTP, stateless). Same admin token.
  if (url.pathname === "/mcp") return handleMcp(request, env);

  // PUT /admin/shared/<version>/<path>: upload one shared asset file.
  if (method === "PUT" && parts[0] === "admin" && parts[1] === "shared" && parts.length > 3) {
    const key = r2SharedKey(parts.slice(2).join("/"));
    await env.SITES.put(key, request.body, {
      httpMetadata: { contentType: request.headers.get("content-type") ?? "application/octet-stream" },
    });
    return json({ key }, 201);
  }

  if (parts[0] !== "sites") throw new HttpError(404, "없는 경로입니다");

  // POST /sites {config, createdBy}
  if (method === "POST" && parts.length === 1) {
    const body = await readJson<{ config: unknown; createdBy?: CreatedBy }>(request);
    return json(await createSiteFromInput(env, body.config, creator(body.createdBy)), 201);
  }

  // GET /sites: every site with its published version number and primary hostname.
  if (method === "GET" && parts.length === 1) {
    return json({ sites: await listSites(env.DB, env.BASE_DOMAIN) });
  }
  if (parts.length < 2) throw new HttpError(404, "없는 경로입니다");

  const site = await getSite(env.DB, parts[1]!);

  if (method === "GET" && parts.length === 2) {
    return json({ site, versions: await listVersions(env.DB, site.id) });
  }

  // PATCH /sites/:id {notifyEmail}: operational settings outside the versioned config.
  if (method === "PATCH" && parts.length === 2) {
    const body = await readJson<{ notifyEmail?: unknown }>(request);
    if ("notifyEmail" in body) {
      await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE sites SET notify_email = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(requireEmail(body.notifyEmail), site.id).run();
    }
    return json({ site: await getSite(env.DB, site.id) });
  }

  // GET /sites/:id/metrics?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD (Korean dates, inclusive; default last 30 days)
  if (method === "GET" && parts[2] === "metrics" && parts.length === 3) {
    const today = kstDay(new Date());
    const from = url.searchParams.get("from") ?? kstDay(new Date(Date.now() - 29 * 86_400_000));
    const to = url.searchParams.get("to") ?? today;
    if (![from, to].every((d) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d))) throw new HttpError(400, "from·to는 YYYY-MM-DD");
    return json(await metricsReport(env.DB, site.id, from, to));
  }

  // GET /sites/:id/inquiries[.csv]?from=&to= (ISO dates)
  if (method === "GET" && (parts[2] === "inquiries" || parts[2] === "inquiries.csv") && parts.length === 3) {
    const range = { from: url.searchParams.get("from") ?? undefined, to: url.searchParams.get("to") ?? undefined };
    if (parts[2] === "inquiries") {
      return json(
        await listInquiries(env, site.id, {
          ...range,
          before: url.searchParams.get("before") ?? undefined,
          limit: Number(url.searchParams.get("limit") ?? 200),
        }),
      );
    }
    return new Response(inquiriesCsv(await allInquiries(env, site.id, range)), {
      headers: {
        "content-type": "text/csv; charset=utf-8",
        "content-disposition": `attachment; filename="inquiries-${site.slug}.csv"`,
      },
    });
  }

  // POST /sites/:id/versions {config, createdBy, note}: new draft version
  if (method === "POST" && parts[2] === "versions") {
    const body = await readJson<{ config: unknown; createdBy?: CreatedBy; note?: string }>(request);
    const result = await addDraft(env, site, body.config, creator(body.createdBy), body.note ?? null);
    return json(result, 201);
  }

  // POST /sites/:id/assets: raw image body, or JSON {url}. GET lists them.
  if (parts[2] === "assets" && parts.length === 3) {
    if (method === "POST") return json(await uploadAsset(env, site, request), 201);
    if (method === "GET") return json({ assets: await listAssets(env, site) });
  }

  // POST /sites/:id/publish {versionId?}: latest version when omitted
  if (method === "POST" && parts[2] === "publish") {
    const body = await readJson<{ versionId?: string }>(request).catch(() => ({}) as { versionId?: string });
    return json(await publish(env, site, body.versionId));
  }

  // POST /sites/:id/suspend | /resume: stop or restart public serving (non-payment, requests).
  if (method === "POST" && (parts[2] === "suspend" || parts[2] === "resume") && parts.length === 3) {
    if (site.cancelled_at) throw new HttpError(409, "해지된 사이트입니다");
    const status = parts[2] === "suspend" ? "suspended" : "active";
    await setSiteStatus(env.DB, site.id, status);
    await syncHostRecordsNow(env, site.id);
    return json({ siteId: site.id, status });
  }

  // POST /sites/:id/rollback {versionId}
  if (method === "POST" && parts[2] === "rollback") {
    const body = await readJson<{ versionId: string }>(request);
    if (!body.versionId) throw new HttpError(400, "versionId가 필요합니다");
    return json(await rollback(env, site, body.versionId));
  }

  throw new HttpError(404, "없는 경로입니다");
}

export { PublicApi } from "./public.js";

export default {
  async scheduled(controller: ScheduledController, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
    ctx.waitUntil(
      (async () => {
        if (controller.cron === AUDIT_CRON) console.log("[routes] audit", JSON.stringify(await auditHostRecords(env)));
        console.log("[routes] reconcile", JSON.stringify(await reconcileHostRecords(env)));
      })(),
    );
  },

  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    try {
      return await handle(request, env);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof HttpError) return json({ error: error.message, details: error.details }, error.status);
      if (error instanceof SiteConfigError) return json({ error: "설정 검증 실패", details: error.errors }, 422);
      console.error(error);
      return json({ error: "서버 오류" }, 500);
    }
  },
};

export class HttpError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: number,
    message: string,
    readonly details?: unknown,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export function json(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body, null, 2), {
    status,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8" },
  });
}

export async function readJson<T>(request: Request): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return (await request.json()) as T;
  } catch {
    throw new HttpError(400, "요청 본문이 JSON이 아닙니다");
  }
}

const encoder = new TextEncoder();
// Workers extension; the DOM lib (needed by the renderer's JSX) does not declare it.
const subtle = crypto.subtle as SubtleCrypto & { timingSafeEqual(a: ArrayBufferView, b: ArrayBufferView): boolean };

/** Bearer token check in constant time. */
export function requireAdmin(request: Request, token: string | undefined) {
  if (!token) throw new HttpError(500, "ADMIN_TOKEN이 설정되지 않았습니다");
  const given = request.headers.get("authorization")?.replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, "") ?? "";
  const a = encoder.encode(given);
  const b = encoder.encode(token);
  if (a.byteLength !== b.byteLength || !subtle.timingSafeEqual(a, b)) {
    throw new HttpError(401, "인증이 필요합니다");
  }
}
import type { SiteConfig } from "@sites/renderer";
import { HttpError } from "./http.js";

export interface SiteRow {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  template_id: string;
  published_version_id: string | null;
  status: string;
  customer_id: string | null;
  notify_email: string | null;
  template_version: string | null;
  cancelled_at: string | null;
  published_at: string | null;
  routes_synced_at: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface VersionRow {
  id: string;
  site_id: string;
  number: number;
  config: string;
  created_by: string;
  note: string | null;
  preview_token: string;
  asset_version: string | null;
  rendered_at: string | null;
  rendered_origin: string | null;
  first_published_at: string | null;
  created_at: string;
}

export type CreatedBy = "ai" | "operator" | "customer";

const now = () => new Date().toISOString();
const token = () => crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, "");

export async function getSite(db: D1Database, id: string): Promise<SiteRow> {
  const site = await db.prepare("SELECT * FROM sites WHERE id = ?").bind(id).first<SiteRow>();
  if (!site) throw new HttpError(404, `사이트가 없습니다: ${id}`);
  return site;
}

export async function getVersion(db: D1Database, siteId: string, versionId: string): Promise<VersionRow> {
  const row = await db
    .prepare("SELECT * FROM site_versions WHERE id = ? AND site_id = ?")
    .bind(versionId, siteId)
    .first<VersionRow>();
  if (!row) throw new HttpError(404, `버전이 없습니다: ${versionId}`);
  return row;
}

export async function latestVersion(db: D1Database, siteId: string): Promise<VersionRow> {
  const row = await db
    .prepare("SELECT * FROM site_versions WHERE site_id = ? ORDER BY number DESC LIMIT 1")
    .bind(siteId)
    .first<VersionRow>();
  if (!row) throw new HttpError(409, "버전이 없습니다");
  return row;
}

export async function listVersions(db: D1Database, siteId: string) {
  const { results } = await db
    .prepare(
      "SELECT id, number, created_by, note, preview_token, asset_version, rendered_at, first_published_at, created_at FROM site_versions WHERE site_id = ? ORDER BY number DESC",
    )
    .bind(siteId)
    .all();
  return results;
}

export async function createSite(db: D1Database, config: SiteConfig, createdBy: CreatedBy, hostname: string) {
  const exists = await db.prepare("SELECT id FROM sites WHERE slug = ?").bind(config.slug).first();
  if (exists) throw new HttpError(409, `이미 쓰는 slug입니다: ${config.slug}`);
  const siteId = crypto.randomUUID();
  const versionId = crypto.randomUUID();
  const at = now();
  await db.batch([
    db
      .prepare("INSERT INTO sites (id, slug, template_id, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)")
      .bind(siteId, config.slug, config.templateId, at, at),
    db
      .prepare(
        "INSERT INTO site_versions (id, site_id, number, config, created_by, note, preview_token, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
      )
      .bind(versionId, siteId, JSON.stringify(config), createdBy, "최초 생성", token(), at),
    db
      .prepare("INSERT INTO domains (hostname, site_id, kind, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, 'subdomain', ?)")
      .bind(hostname, siteId, at),
  ]);
  return { siteId, versionId };
}

export async function addVersion(
  db: D1Database,
  site: SiteRow,
  config: SiteConfig,
  createdBy: CreatedBy,
  note: string | null,
) {
  if (config.slug !== site.slug) throw new HttpError(422, "slug는 바꿀 수 없습니다", [`slug: ${site.slug}`]);
  const latest = await latestVersion(db, site.id);
  const versionId = crypto.randomUUID();
  const at = now();
  await db.batch([
    db
      .prepare(
        "INSERT INTO site_versions (id, site_id, number, config, created_by, note, preview_token, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
      )
      .bind(versionId, site.id, latest.number + 1, JSON.stringify(config), createdBy, note, token(), at),
    db.prepare("UPDATE sites SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(at, site.id),
  ]);
  return { versionId, number: latest.number + 1 };
}

/** `renderKey` (stored in rendered_origin): origin and other render inputs outside the config. */
export async function markRendered(db: D1Database, versionId: string, assetVersion: string, renderKey: string) {
  await db
    .prepare("UPDATE site_versions SET rendered_at = ?, asset_version = ?, rendered_origin = ? WHERE id = ?")
    .bind(now(), assetVersion, renderKey, versionId)
    .run();
}

export async function markPublished(db: D1Database, siteId: string, versionId: string, templateVersion: string) {
  const at = now();
  await db.batch([
    db
      .prepare("UPDATE site_versions SET first_published_at = COALESCE(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?")
      .bind(at, versionId),
    db
      .prepare(
        "UPDATE sites SET published_version_id = ?, published_at = ?, template_version = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
      )
      .bind(versionId, at, templateVersion, at, siteId),
  ]);
  return at;
}

/** Active custom domain if any, else the default subdomain. */
export async function primaryHostname(db: D1Database, site: SiteRow, baseDomain: string): Promise<string> {
  const custom = await db
    .prepare("SELECT hostname FROM domains WHERE site_id = ? AND kind = 'custom' AND status = 'active' ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 1")
    .bind(site.id)
    .first<{ hostname: string }>();
  return custom?.hostname ?? `${site.slug}.${baseDomain}`;
}

export async function versionByPreviewToken(db: D1Database, previewToken: string): Promise<VersionRow> {
  const row = await db
    .prepare("SELECT * FROM site_versions WHERE preview_token = ?")
    .bind(previewToken)
    .first<VersionRow>();
  if (!row) throw new HttpError(404, "프리뷰가 없습니다");
  return row;
}

export async function setSiteStatus(db: D1Database, siteId: string, status: "active" | "suspended") {
  await db.prepare("UPDATE sites SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(status, now(), siteId).run();
}

/** Authoritative serving state from D1: site row plus the published version, if any. */
export async function servingState(db: D1Database, siteId: string) {
  const site = await getSite(db, siteId);
  const version = site.published_version_id
    ? await db
        .prepare("SELECT id, number, config FROM site_versions WHERE id = ?")
        .bind(site.published_version_id)
        .first<{ id: string; number: number; config: string }>()
    : null;
  return { site, version };
}

/** Records that KV host records reflect the site as of `updatedAt` (no-op if the row moved on). */
export async function markRoutesSynced(db: D1Database, siteId: string, updatedAt: string) {
  await db
    .prepare("UPDATE sites SET routes_synced_at = ? WHERE id = ? AND updated_at = ?")
    .bind(updatedAt, siteId, updatedAt)
    .run();
}

/** Sites whose KV host records may lag D1. */
export async function sitesNeedingRouteSync(db: D1Database, limit = 100) {
  const { results } = await db
    .prepare("SELECT id FROM sites WHERE routes_synced_at IS NULL OR routes_synced_at < updated_at ORDER BY updated_at LIMIT ?")
    .bind(limit)
    .all<{ id: string }>();
  return results.map((row) => row.id);
}
export interface Env {
  DB: D1Database;
  SITES: R2Bucket;
  ROUTES: KVNamespace;
  BASE_DOMAIN: string;
  ADMIN_TOKEN: string;
  /** `https` in deployed environments, `http` locally. Used for canonical and OG URLs. */
  PUBLIC_SCHEME?: string;
  /** Public origin of this API (`https://api.dsites.kr`); hosts the Turnstile frame sites embed. */
  API_ORIGIN?: string;
  /** Turnstile widget key (public) and secret. Without a secret the check is skipped. */
  TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY?: string;
  TURNSTILE_SECRET?: string;
  /** `binding` (Cloudflare Email Service), `mailpit` (local inbox) or `off`. */
  MAIL_MODE?: "binding" | "mailpit" | "off";
  MAIL_FROM?: string;
  MAIL_PIT_URL?: string;
  EMAIL?: SendEmail;
  /** Pepper for hashing visitor IPs (rate limiting without storing addresses). */
  HASH_PEPPER?: string;
}
